# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-19 through 2026-11-25  
**Updated:** 2026-11-19T10:57:28+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-19

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-22 — Welcome to Chinatown Holiday Market: opening Sunday + June Baby

**Welcome to Chinatown Holiday Market: opening Sunday, then a meal.** Filipino-inspired cocktails and small plates at June Baby. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=115+Bowery%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=197+Second+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://welcometochinatown.com/holiday-market-2026) · [Event booking](https://luma.com/welcometo-z8gr) · [Restaurant](https://www.instagram.com/junebaby.nyc/)

### 2026-11-20 — Brasserie Boulud + The Danish Girl in Concert

**Dinner, then The Danish Girl in Concert.** French brasserie at Brasserie Boulud. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. The Danish Girl in Concert starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=1887+Broadway&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/the-danish-girl-in-concert) · [Event booking](https://tickets.lincolncenter.org/en/booking/production/33631) · [Restaurant](https://www.brasserieboulud.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/brasserie-boulud-nyc)

### 2026-11-21 — Barnard/Columbia Dances — Saturday Matinee + June Baby

**Barnard/Columbia Dances — Saturday Matinee, then a meal.** Filipino-inspired cocktails and small plates at June Baby. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=219+West+19th+Street&destination=197+Second+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://newyorklivearts.org/event/barnard-columbia-dances-at-new-york-live-arts-5/) · [Event booking](https://newyorklivearts.my.salesforce-sites.com/ticket/#/events/a0SVt00000gDW57MAG) · [Restaurant](https://www.instagram.com/junebaby.nyc/)

### 2026-11-19 — North by Northwest + Sam's Restaurant

**North by Northwest, then dinner.** Italian American at Sam's Restaurant. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=238+Court+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/north-by-northwest/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24510003/) · [Restaurant](https://www.samsbrooklyn.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.opentable.com/booking/restref/availability?rid=1505083)

### 2026-11-23 — SKATE at Domino Park + Sweetwater

**SKATE at Domino Park, then a meal.** Neighborhood bistro at Sweetwater. It is a 10-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=South+3rd+Street+entrance+between+Kent+Avenue+and+River+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=105+North+6th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating) · [Event booking](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating/buy-tickets) · [Restaurant](https://www.sweetwaterny.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/ny/sweetwater-restaurant)

### 2026-11-20 — Lightscape: opening value night + SSRP

**Lightscape: opening value night, then dinner.** Japanese ramen at SSRP. It is a 16-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 4:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=565+Vanderbilt+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape) · [Event booking](https://events.leapevents.com/tickets/lightscape2026/listing/) · [Restaurant](https://www.ssrpnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/ssrp)

### 2026-11-21 — The Piano — 4K restoration brunch screening + Altbau

**The Piano — 4K restoration brunch screening, then a meal.** Bakery and coffee at Altbau. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=136+Metropolitan+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/williamsburg/movies/the-piano/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/williamsburg/purchase/24710424/) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-19 — Altbau + Tethering: multiplayer games and Smush Bois tournament

**Dinner, then Tethering: multiplayer games and Smush Bois tournament.** Bakery and coffee at Altbau. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 6 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=47+Great+Jones+Street%2C+Third+Floor%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.culturehub.org/events/tethering-an-arcade-with-deep-soul-ties) · [Event booking](https://pools.events/event/jIw1Zz6t/tethering-an-arcade-with-deep-soul-ties/?i=MXDcQ47E) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

35mm Import. A pinnacle of the art of filmic portraiture, Gregory Markopoulos’s GALAXIE comprises thirty short portraits of men and women from across the spectrum of the mid-1960s cultural scene in New York City. ## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 19: everyday deals, recent openings, Thanksgiving menus and temporary pie and omurice stops. Unconfirmed dates, holiday service and charges are flagged.

Dining research: 2026-11-19T10:42:09+00:00

No current Michelin stars or new awards were independently verified for these candidates. Junoon's historical-star marketing is not evidence of a current award, and guide inclusion must not be presented as a star. Recognition fields remain empty. No primary Michelin page was checked; research used the supplied captures and 12 direct follow-up requests.

### Amélie: $1.50 oysters, $10 small plates and a $26 early-bird meal

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

The restaurant's current happy-hour menu offers oysters, French small plates and burger-and-fries or moules-frites with beer or a glass of wine.

Price: $1.50 oysters; $10 selected small plates and cocktails; $7 beers; $14 three-wine flight; $19 six oysters with a glass of wine; $26 early-bird meal with a drink.  
Schedule: Daily: food 4–6pm; drinks from opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Selected drinks and dishes only. Oyster minimum, seating restrictions, reservation requirement, tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No expiration stated. Holiday hours unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Amélie: $26 two-course weekday lunch

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A separate daytime set menu offers two courses, with choices including French starters, chicken paillard, croque monsieur and moules frites.

Price: $26 for two courses; wine of the day +$10; burger +$5; steak frites or Niçoise +$8.  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, noon–3:30pm.  
Conditions: Tax and gratuity explicitly excluded. Additional supplements apply to selected options. Complimentary bread basket; additional basket $3. Reservation requirement and holiday exceptions are not stated. No expiration stated.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### Junoon: $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The Prestige lunch includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian, seafood and meat choices such as mushroom xacutti, shrimp moilee and goat-shank lal maas.

Price: $43 for two courses; $49 for three courses.  
Schedule: Friday–Sunday lunch, 11:45am–2:30pm, according to the restaurant-linked location FAQ; holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
Conditions: The menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Extra accompaniments are separately priced; tax and other gratuity treatment are unspecified. The restaurant-linked FAQ recommends reservations, welcomes children above age 10 and specifies smart-casual dress without gym shorts, tank tops or flip-flops.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Frenzie: late-night pizza-and-drink bundles from $40

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

A late-night cheese-pie bundle offers a straightforward shared meal with beer, daiquiris or martinis.

Price: $40 cheese pie and pitcher of Narragansett; $45 cheese pie and two daiquiris; $50 cheese pie and two martinis; pepperoni +$4.  
Schedule: Thursday–Saturday after 10pm; ending time not stated.  
Conditions: Walk-in only. Menu subject to seasonal availability. Takeout eligibility for these bundles is unconfirmed; the site's separate takeout policy is limited. Tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. Holiday exceptions and expiration unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

### Mercato Centrale: $1 morning drip coffee

Deals & happy hours — Mercato Centrale

A low-cost morning stop inside Grand Central offers La Colombe drip coffee for $1.

Price: $1 La Colombe drip coffee.  
Schedule: Daily, 7–11am. Market regular hours: 7am–11pm.  
Conditions: Limit one per person. Walk-in market; no reservation needed. No additional purchase requirement or expiration stated. Tax treatment and holiday exceptions are not stated.  
[Source](https://www.centralenyc.com/mercato)

### Miss Lily's 7A: $25 brunch drinks add-on

Deals & happy hours — Miss Lily's 7A

Weekend brunch offers unlimited selected drinks as an add-on to a separately purchased signature dish.

Price: $25 per person for the drinks add-on; required signature dishes are separately priced at $18–$33 on the checked menu.  
Schedule: Saturday and Sunday brunch, noon–4pm.  
Conditions: A separately purchased signature dish is required; $25 covers selected unlimited drinks, not the meal. Duration and whole-table participation are unspecified in the readable brunch menu. The restaurant's October 5 dinner PDF explicitly discloses an automatic 18% gratuity, with additional tips optional; whether the same policy applies at brunch was not verified. Tax, other charges and holiday exceptions remain unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://v1-misslilys.getbento.com/east-village-7a/)

### Burger Village Fresh Meadows: $17 weekday lunch at a new location

Deals & happy hours — Burger Village Fresh Meadows

The newly opened location offers a burger or grilled-chicken sandwich with fries and soda, or selected tacos with chips and soda. Eater reports a November 16 opening.

Price: $17 lunch special; draft beer +$5.  
Schedule: Monday–Thursday, 11am–4pm. Regular restaurant hours: Monday–Wednesday 11am–11pm; Thursday–Sunday 11am–midnight.  
Conditions: Not available on national holidays, including Thanksgiving on November 26. The unavailable date below concerns the lunch offer, not a confirmed restaurant closure. Selected combinations only; tax, gratuity, service charges, takeout eligibility and reservation requirement are not stated.  
Opened: 2026-11-16  
[Source](https://www.burgervillage.com/fresh-meadows/)

### Sushiro: announced November 19 debut with $2 otoro

New openings — Sushiro Times Square

The chain announces its first US location for November 19, with a $2 otoro grand-opening special.

Price: $2 otoro grand-opening special.  
Schedule: Announced opening November 19; listed daily hours 11am–11pm, last orders 10:30pm.  
Conditions: While supplies last. Promotion end date, quantity limit, minimum purchase, tax and service charges are not stated. A public waitlist link is available; no place was joined and live availability was not checked. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
From: 2026-11-19  
Published: 2026-11-18  
[Source](https://sushiro-usa.com/promotion)

### Café d'Alsace: $95 Thanksgiving prix fixe

Special menus — Café d'Alsace

The holiday menu offers three courses, with turkey, salmon, braised short rib or mushroom risotto among the mains.

Price: $95 three-course prix fixe; $32 turkey plate for children 12 and under.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving menu, 2–9pm; the posted menu does not specify a year or calendar date.  
Conditions: The $32 children's price covers a turkey plate for ages 12 and under, not the adult three-course menu. The posted menu's applicability to Thanksgiving 2026 requires confirmation. Tax, gratuity, service charges, booking requirements and live availability are unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.cafedalsace.com/menus/)

### Russian Tea Room: Thanksgiving with Russian alternatives to turkey

Special menus — The Russian Tea Room

The three-course holiday menu includes turkey alongside mushroom stroganoff, salmon-and-lobster kulebyaka and beef stroganoff.

Price: $145 per person; $95 for children 12 and under.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving, November 26; meal seating times unconfirmed.  
Conditions: Tax, gratuity, service charges and booking requirements are not stated in the checked menu. Live availability unconfirmed. Separate afternoon-tea times should not be treated as Thanksgiving dinner seatings.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://russiantearoomnyc.com/thanksgiving/)

### Le Rock: $185 Thanksgiving supper

Special menus — Le Rock

The holiday menu combines individual course choices with shared sides; mains include heritage turkey, halibut, filet au poivre and wild-mushroom rice pilaf.

Price: $185 for the official menu's 'Three + Courses'.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving, November 26; exact seating times unconfirmed.  
Conditions: Tax, gratuity, service charges and cancellation terms were not verified. Live availability unconfirmed. Regular November Sunday closure does not apply to this Thursday event; regular hours are not assumed to be holiday seating hours.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)

### Red Rooster Harlem: Thanksgiving feast with live music

Special menus — Red Rooster Harlem

Eater reports a family-style Thanksgiving meal with fried or roasted turkey, cornbread stuffing and pecan pie, plus music on two levels.

Price: $90 family-style meal, as reported by Eater; charging unit and full inclusions require confirmation.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving, November 26; exact service times unknown.  
Conditions: Family-style versus à la carte service depends on the floor. Party minimum, tax, gratuity, service charges and cancellation rules are unconfirmed. Official PDF pricing could not be read; treat the editorial price as needing reconfirmation before booking.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
Published: 2026-11-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-thanksgiving-restaurants-nyc)

New openings — Garnet

The former Clover Hill space has become a casual canteen with communal dining, lunch trays, soups and stews. Dinner is planned for early December, not assumed available now.

Schedule: Wednesday–Sunday, 10am–3pm.  
Conditions: Current daytime service only. Dinner launch date, reservation policy, holiday exceptions and live availability are unconfirmed.  
Opened: 2026-11-18  
Published: 2026-11-18

Chef Stephanie Bonnin's new supper club serves a personalized multicourse dinner at a single ten-seat table.

Schedule: Friday and Saturday, one seating each night; exact seating time unconfirmed.  
Conditions: Small-capacity reservation format; live availability, tax, gratuity, service charges and cancellation terms unconfirmed. This is an ongoing restaurant opening, not a finite pop-up or a verified guest-chef collaboration.

### Brasserie Boulud: French classics near Lincoln Center

New openings — Brasserie Boulud

Daniel Boulud's consolidated brasserie opened in October, replacing his earlier restaurant configuration at this address. The current menu includes burgers, roast chicken, trout and coq au vin.

Price: Classic burger $25; trout $30; branzino $32; coq au vin $36; half roast chicken $39.  
Schedule: Tuesday–Sunday, 4:30–10:30pm, according to the checked official page.  
Conditions: These are ordinary à la carte prices, not a promotion. Exact opening day, holiday exceptions, tax, gratuity and service charges unconfirmed. Do not assume all-day service from earlier editorial descriptions.

### Creola: September opening focused on Black-diaspora tasting menus
